HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B002","root_001696/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_279f56316afce6a2ceda","text":"4. **The mark of certainty.** Branch evidence: ع ل م B002, distinguishing mark; ي ق ن B001, removal of doubt; ر ء ي B011/B012, visible marker and showing. Structural cue: knowledge is named twice in the ayah, then the object is shown. Assigned role: علم B002 turns certainty into a discriminating sign that marks reality before sight confirms it. Abductive move: I infer that true certainty leaves a visible orientation-marker in conduct. Reading change: the ayah reads as \"if your lives bore the mark of certainty,\" not only \"if you possessed information.\"","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2c606018125c99b6320c","text":"3. **Knowledge as a gathered reservoir against heat.** Branch evidence: ع ل م B005, large gathered water; ع ي ن B006, springing water-eye; ج ح م B001, blazing fire/intense heat. Structural cue: the phrase joins علم to يقين just before the promised sight of الجحيم and then عين اليقين. Assigned role: the water branches give علم/عين a material counter-image to fire. Abductive move: I infer that the packet's later heat retroactivates water-like knowledge as what would have cooled or answered it. Reading change: \"علم اليقين\" can read not only as epistemic certainty but as a stored, life-preserving resource lacking before the blaze appears.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001696/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_33f5ead824c33f0145ab","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:5:5\",\"root_001696\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Kesinliğin içte korunmuş bir sığınak oluşturduğu ve sonraki çıplak görüşe karşı durduğu okuması mümkündür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B002","root_001696/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_550ddac7b27381043dca","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:5:3\",\"root_001040\",[\"B002\"]],[\"102:5:4\",\"root_001040\",[\"B002\"]],[\"102:5:5\",\"root_001696\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Buradaki kesinlik, doğrudan karşılaşmadan önce ayırt edici işaretlerle yerleşen bilgi; sonraki göz kesinliği ise aynı gerçeğin yüz yüze doğrulanmasıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5946b1c6a0ca53c9b576","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:5:3\",\"root_001040\",[\"B005\"]],[\"102:5:4\",\"root_001040\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Bilgi, yaklaşan ateşin hararetine karşı biriktirilmiş hayat verici bir kaynak gibi okunabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B001","root_001696/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6d7b1dfa967667655ebb","text":"2. **Certainty as protected interior.** Branch evidence: ي ق ن B002, protected secluded girl; ق ب ر B002, hiddenness and inward sinking; ر ح م B003, womb; ع ل م B001, inner apprehension. Structural cue: the ayah speaks of \"knowledge of certainty\" before the later eye-certainty; earlier and later packet images include womb, grave, and direct exposure. Assigned role: يقين B002 supplies protected interiority; عين اليقين later supplies exposure. Abductive move: I infer a contrast between a guarded inward certainty and the later compulsory public sight. Reading change: the ayah can read as an inner, sheltered certainty that could have lived before exposure forced it. This is exploratory because يقين B002 is a narrow nominal branch, but the concealment/protection relation is structurally active.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001696/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_70bada4ab8263d168b76","text":"102:7's replacement of `عِلْمَ ٱلْيَقِينِ` with `عَيْنَ ٱلْيَقِينِ` clarified the main relation: the contrast is between sign-mediated and face-to-face modes, not between uncertainty and certainty, since fixed `ي ق ن B001` is settled in both. 102:8 then made the omitted consequence of `لَوْ` newly practical: knowledge that has truly settled should affect conduct before favors become matters of account.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B002","root_001069/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_73d3aa175e09fcd7af19","text":"2. **Certain knowledge through a distinguishing sign, before face-to-face encounter.** Fixed `ع ل م B002` contributes a mark or landmark that points to its referent, while fixed `ي ق ن B001` makes the resulting knowledge settled rather than conjectural. Structurally, the graves have already formed a boundary marker, and this phrase stands immediately before seeing in 102:6 and `عَيْنَ ٱلْيَقِينِ` in 102:7, where contextual `ع ي ن B002` supplies direct encounter. The `عِلْم` phrase is assigned a sign-mediated role and the `عَيْن` phrase an eyewitness role. I infer those epistemic stages from sequence and lexical contrast; the packet does not rank one as less certain. The fixed ayah therefore names certainty available through intelligible marks before the same certainty becomes directly manifest.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7dee8a84104d85343089","text":"102:6 made the water/reservoir reading of ع ل م B005 more surprising: it only became visible when الجحيم supplied intense heat immediately afterward. 102:7 then split certainty into two modes, علم اليقين and عين اليقين, so the counterfactual knowledge in 102:5 reads as an inward stabilizer that precedes forced outward sight. 102:8 made this inwardness ethically active: if certainty had been present, it would have changed the use of نعيم before the later question.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001040/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b73b2f31ff81eddbc657","text":"3. **An offered epistemic exit from the sequence.** Fixed `ع ل م B001` includes informing and teaching, and fixed `ي ق ن B001` includes verification. The full window places this conditional after merciful naming (`ر ح م B001`) but before forced sight and final questioning. Teaching is assigned the role of advance disclosure that could end diversion voluntarily; seeing supplies the later compulsory verification. I infer that prior warning is an act of beneficence and that the unrealized condition marks failure to take it up. The conditional `لَوْ`, following diversion and rivalry, leaves its consequence unstated; the next two ayat supply emphatic sight. Knowledge is assigned the role of interrupting the behavior that `ل ه و B001` and `ك ث ر B002` described. I supply the omitted causal consequence: if knowledge were genuinely settled, it would break the attention loop before compulsory sight.
